# Evidence ledger: fc_uk_mortgages_2026_09

- Question: Assume today is 2026-10-27. You know nothing published after this date. Estimate the number of net mortgage approvals for house purchase in the United Kingdom in September 2026 (seasonally adjusted), as it will be first reported in the Bank of England's monthly Money and Credit statistical release.
- as_of: 2026-10-27
- Pinned target: Number of net mortgage approvals for house purchase in the United Kingdom in September 2026, seasonally adjusted, as first reported by the Bank of England in its monthly Money and Credit statistical release | unit net mortgage approvals for house purchase, seasonally adjusted | period 2026-09-01..2026-09-30 | publisher Bank of England

## Directions and pages

### d1 [own_history] Bank of England statistics

Hypothesis: The target's own recent history and September seasonality provide the primary anchor: September 2026 approvals should be close to the recent seasonally adjusted level adjusted by the historical September seasonal factor. Route: target ≈ recent 3-month average × historical September factor, with recent trend adjustment.

### d2 [related_series] Surveyors and mortgage lenders

### d4 [related_series] Bank of England monetary statistics

Hypothesis: Mortgage approvals for house purchase co-move with remortgage approvals and are negatively related to new mortgage effective interest rates. August 2026 remortgage approvals, net mortgage lending, and effective rates can constrain the September 2026 house purchase approvals level. Route: target ≈ f(remortgage approvals, effective new mortgage rate change, net lending).

Queries issued by the searcher: Bank of England Money and Credit August 2026 mortgage approvals effective interest rate; UK effective mortgage interest rate September 2026 Bank of England; UK 5-year swap rate September 2026 mortgages; "Money and Credit" Bank of England July 2026 mortgage approvals 55,900 remortgaging 34,600; Bank of England Money and Credit June 2026 mortgage approvals net borrowing; mortgage approvals forecast September 2026 economists expect; Bank of England Money and Credit mortgage approvals August 2026 effective interest rate new mortgages; UK net mortgage approvals house purchase September 2026 Bank of England; Bank of England Money and Credit August 2026 statistical release remortgage approvals net mortgage lending; UK 5-year swap rate September 2026 mortgages gilt yields; Bank of England Money and Credit mortgage approvals August 2026 effective interest rate new mortgages; UK net mortgage approvals house purchase 2026 Bank of England; bankofengland.co.uk statistics money-and-credit 2026 august-2026; Bank Rate decision September 2026 Bank of England monetary policy committee; UK 5-year swap rate September 2026 gilt yields rise mortgage rates 5%

### d5 [administrative] HMRC property transaction records

Hypothesis: Residential property transactions are an administrative trace of completed housing activity. Although approvals lead completions, the recent level and trend in HMRC transactions and stamp duty receipts can validate whether the housing market was stronger or weaker in September 2026. Route: use transactions trend to adjust the approvals forecast, not as a direct one-to-one substitute.

### d6 [capacity_consumption] Mortgage lenders and product market

Hypothesis: Mortgage product availability, lender appetite, and headline fixed mortgage rates affect approval volumes. A rise in product count and fall in rates should support approvals; a sharp tightening should suppress them. Route: target ≈ baseline approvals × product/rate adjustment factor.

Queries issued by the searcher: Moneyfacts average 2-year fixed mortgage rate October 2026 product count; Bank of England mortgage approvals August 2026 Money and Credit; "mortgage approvals" September 2026 Bank of England money and credit October 2026; UK Finance mortgage lending August 2026 household finance update applications; Moneyfacts mortgage product count September 2026 average 2-year fixed rate; UK mortgage rates October 2026 product availability lenders; Bank of England mortgage approvals August 2026 net approvals house purchase Money and Credit; UK Finance mortgage lending indicators September 2026 applications; Moneyfacts mortgage product count September 2026 average 2-year fixed rate; UK mortgage rates October 2026 product availability lenders; Bank of England net mortgage approvals August 2026 Money and Credit; UK Finance mortgage lending August 2026 house purchase approvals

### b1 [related_series] historical calibration

Hypothesis: A monthly regression of BoE house-purchase approvals on RICS buyer enquiries and mortgage-rate/product data would replace the judgemental September adjustment factors a1/a2 in m2-m3, reducing the largest uncertainty and unlocking a cleaner survey-based route.

### b2 [capacity_consumption] rate-shock conversion

Hypothesis: A historical elasticity of approvals to new mortgage rates or product shelf-life would let us convert the observed September tightening (average new rate 5.55% to 5.83%; two-year fixed around 5.59% in September; sub-5% deals collapsing) into an approvals adjustment rather than relying on a broad assumption.

## Methods (computed by code)

| id | method | estimate | inputs (points / assumptions) | backtest years | mean abs ln err | weight | weight basis | notes / error |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| m1 | formula_mc | 54,012 | ['p16'] / ['a3'] | [] |  | 0.50 | B1 of 2 blocks; geometric mean of blocks |  |
| m2 | formula_mc | 54,687 | ['p1', 'p2', 'p197'] / ['a1'] | [] |  | 0.50 | B2 of 2 blocks; geometric mean of blocks |  |

- m1 coverage: the whole target
- m2 coverage: the whole target

Method details (computed by code):

- m1 formula_mc: p16 * a3; Monte Carlo P10 to P90 36,584 to 80,517; result unit (approval/month) * 1 month converted to approval; own 80% width x/÷1.48 (Monte Carlo of the formula inputs)
- m2 formula_mc: mean(p1, p2, p197) * a1; Monte Carlo P10 to P90 37,909 to 78,513; result unit (approval/month) * 1 month converted to approval; own 80% width x/÷1.44 (Monte Carlo of the formula inputs)
- Combination: 2 block(s), equal weight per block (methods whose inputs overlap by at least half form one block; backtest errors set widths, not weights): B1 = m1: 54,012 (width x/÷1.48, Monte Carlo of the formula inputs); B2 = m2: 54,687 (width x/÷1.44, Monte Carlo of the formula inputs). Estimate = the geometric mean of the two blocks.
- Interval: root mean square of the block widths x/÷1.46; Birge ratio 0.03 (not above one, so no widening); final width x/÷1.46.

Result: central **54,348** approval, 80% interval [37,184, 79,436] approval, basis blocks (several routes with separate inputs: the width is the typical width of the routes' own intervals, not narrowed for their number), weight rule equal_blocks_geometric_mean, weight on methods that use assumptions 1.00

Unit of the estimate: approval (net mortgage approvals for house purchase, seasonally adjusted; total for 2026-09-01 to 2026-09-30)

## Final answer

The best estimate for net mortgage approvals for house purchase in the United Kingdom in September 2026, seasonally adjusted, as first reported by the Bank of England, is 54,348 approval (80% model-based interval 37,184 to 79,436 approval).

Two routes were combined with equal weight per block: a year-on-year level-scaling route (54,012 approval, weight 50%) multiplying the September 2025 observed level 65,900 net mortgage approvals for house purchase by an assumed ratio 0.825 (range 0.5821 to 1.164); and a recent-level route (54,687 approval, weight 50%) averaging observed June-August prints 58,200 net mortgage approvals for house purchase, 55,900 net mortgage approvals for house purchase and 54,900 net mortgage approvals for house purchase, adjusted by an assumed factor 0.965 (range 0.6803 to 1.361). The combination is a geometric mean of the two blocks; the interval is conditional on those assumptions.

The main risk is that the assumed September adjustment and year-on-year ratio are wrong, since both rest on survey and rate evidence rather than observed September data. The most useful missing evidence would be early September approvals or a firmer published consensus.

Unit of the estimate: approval (net mortgage approvals for house purchase, seasonally adjusted; total for 2026-09-01 to 2026-09-30).

Key assumptions (proposed by the analyst model, not observed; code samples each one between the bounds shown):
- a1 September 2026 approvals adjustment relative to the June-August 2026 average: 0.965 ratio, range 0.6803 to 1.361 (range widened by code); used by m2. Overturned by: Evidence that RICS/CCS weakness did not transmit to approvals, or that mortgage rates fell or product availability expanded enough to offset the weakening.
- a3 September 2026 net house-purchase approvals relative to September 2025: 0.825 ratio, range 0.5821 to 1.164 (range widened by code); used by m1. Overturned by: Evidence that September 2026 approvals held near or above the summer 2026 level, or that the September 2025 base is materially revised.

Method details (computed by code):
- m1 formula_mc: p16 * a3; Monte Carlo P10 to P90 36,584 to 80,517; result unit (approval/month) * 1 month converted to approval; own 80% width x/÷1.48 (Monte Carlo of the formula inputs)
- m2 formula_mc: mean(p1, p2, p197) * a1; Monte Carlo P10 to P90 37,909 to 78,513; result unit (approval/month) * 1 month converted to approval; own 80% width x/÷1.44 (Monte Carlo of the formula inputs)
- Combination: 2 block(s), equal weight per block (methods whose inputs overlap by at least half form one block; backtest errors set widths, not weights): B1 = m1: 54,012 (width x/÷1.48, Monte Carlo of the formula inputs); B2 = m2: 54,687 (width x/÷1.44, Monte Carlo of the formula inputs). Estimate = the geometric mean of the two blocks.
- Interval: root mean square of the block widths x/÷1.46; Birge ratio 0.03 (not above one, so no widening); final width x/÷1.46.
